  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  哎呀，你落入深渊了！深渊是个特殊的地方：你无法记住或绘出走过的区域；这里到处都是危险的怪物，你很可能会死。

  为了提高你在找到出口<console>（闪烁的 <w>$1</w>）</console>前活下来的机会，要一直移动，不要和任何怪物交战，也不要去追逐地上的物品。如果怪物正在逼近，试着使用物品和能力脱身。
  ```

- `hint:hint_auto_exclusion`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  为防止自动旅行或自动探索把你带入危险区域，可以进入地图视图（<w>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_MAP]</w>），然后在怪物位置按 <w>$cmd[CMD_MAP_EXCLUDE_AREA]</w> 切换排除半径。为了简化操作，<w>auto_exclude</w> 选项列出的一些无法移动的怪物（例如眼前这个）会被视为足够危险，系统会自动为它们设置禁区。杀死怪物后，该禁区会自动清除。你也可以按 <w>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_MAP]</w> 进入地图，再按两次 <w>$cmd[CMD_MAP_EXCLUDE_AREA]</w> 手动移除禁区；但除非把这种怪物从 <w>auto_exclude</w> 列表中移除，否则下一回合禁区还会重新出现。
  ```

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  糟了，$1 因你的虔诚值太低而越来越不悦。这可能是因为你做了亵渎之举，也可能是因为 $1 认为你的崇拜不够虔诚，或两者兼有。如果虔诚值降到零，你就会被逐出教门。赶紧设法提高虔诚值，或停止惹怒你的神祇，必要时两者都做。不管怎样，你最好重新阅读宗教说明。按 <w>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_RELIGION]</w><localtiles>，或按住 <w>Shift</w> 并<w>右键点击</w>你的头像</localtiles>即可查看。
  ```

- `hint:hint_invisible_danger`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  与看不见的怪物战斗很困难。如果你没有能看见隐形生物的手段，可以把隐形怪物引到浅水、不透明的云雾（例如迷雾卷轴制造的云雾）或走廊中；在这些地方，它们的行动更容易判断。如果情况变糟，试着传送离开，或者干脆离开这一层！
  ```

- `hint:hint_miscast_contamination_and_mp`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  如果施法成功率很高（可通过按 <w>$cmd[CMD_CAST_SPELL]\?</w> 或
  <w>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_SPELLS]</w> 查看），那么施法失误只会使法术未能生效，并伴随一个无害的副作用。
  但失败率较高的法术在施法失误时，可能使你受到魔法能量污染，并可能额外产生一个有害副作用。通常这并不是问题，因为魔法污染会随时间消退；但如果你在短时间内反复受到污染，就会发生变异或遭受其他有害影响。

  请注意，施法失误仍会消耗与成功施放该法术时相同的全部法力。
  ```

- `hint:hint_need_healing`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  如果你的生命值或法力不足，而且没有必须立刻移动的理由，可以休息片刻。最好先退到已经探索过并清除了怪物的区域；在已探索区域的边缘休息，更容易被游荡的怪物打断。按 <w>$cmd[CMD_REST]</w> 或 <w>shift-numpad-5</w><localtiles>，也可以点击“休息”按钮</localtiles>来休息。
  ```

- `hint:hint_need_poison_healing`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  你中毒已深，足以致命，现在最好按 <w>$cmd[CMD_QUAFF]</w> 饮用一瓶治疗创伤药水；更好的选择是一瓶治疗药水。如果此前两者都没见过，就试试物品栏里的未知药水。祝你好运！
  ```

- `hint:hint_new_level`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  做得好！提升经验等级总是件令人高兴的事。你的生命值和法力都会增加，力量、智力和敏捷属性偶尔也会提升。

  {{
      if crawl.hints_type() == "magic" then
          return "提升经验等级也能让你学习更难的法术。你可以按"
                 .. " <w>$cmd[CMD_MEMORISE_SPELL]</w> 记忆第二个法术<tiles>，也可以在"
                 .. "法术记忆标签页中<w>单击</w>该法术</tiles>。"
      end
  }}
  ```

- `hint:hint_on_fire`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  你身上沾满了黏着的液态火焰。不断移动可以让它更快熄灭；走进附近的水池则能将它彻底扑灭。不过，在你四处奔跑时，那个让你沾上液态火焰的东西可能会让火势进一步加重。
  ```

- `hint:hint_opportunity_attack`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  当你远离敌人时，它有较小的（1/3）几率趁机发动一次免费攻击。调整站位或撤退通常仍是好选择，但要留意这些额外攻击。
  ```

- `hint:hint_retreat_caster`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  法力耗尽时，你无法施法。虽然仍可进行近战，但那并非你的强项，因此最好尽可能撤退。
  ```

- `hint:hint_run_away_1`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  当生命值很低、随时可能死亡时，要仔细审视自己的选择。撤退或使用某件物品，往往比继续战斗更可行。
  ```

- `hint:hint_run_away_2`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  如果要撤退到另一层，请记住：当你开始上楼或下楼时，紧挨着你的怪物可能会跟过来。即使你已经甩掉了它们，它们仍会留在那里等你回来，因此返回时或许应该改走另一处楼梯。
  ```

- `hint:hint_seen_altar`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  那个<console> $1</console>是一座祭坛。
  <localtiles>用鼠标<w>右键点击</w>它</localtiles>
  <nomouse>如果先用 <w>$cmd[CMD_LOOK_AROUND]</w> 瞄准祭坛，再按
  <w>$cmd[CMD_TARGET_DESCRIBE]</w></nomouse>，就能查看简短说明。

  站在祭坛所在格上时，按 <w>$cmd[CMD_GO_UPSTAIRS]</w> 或 <w>$cmd[CMD_GO_DOWNSTAIRS]</w> 可以加入该信仰，或阅读有关对应神祇的信息。在皈依对应信仰之前，游戏会要求你确认。

  {{
      if you.god() == "No God" and crawl.hints_type() == "magic" then
          return "对缺乏经验的咒法师而言，最容易上手的神祇可能是"
                 .. "维胡梅特，不过包括西芙·穆娜在内的其他许多神祇"
                 .. "也同样合适。"
      end
  }}
  ```

- `hint:hint_seen_armour`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  这是你捡到的第一件护甲<console>（“<w>$item[armour]</w>”）</console>。<localtiles>单击它即可穿戴，再次单击即可卸下。<w>右键单击</w>可查看更多信息。
  </localtiles><nomouse>按 <w>$cmd[CMD_WEAR_ARMOUR]</w> 穿戴，按 <w>$cmd[CMD_REMOVE_ARMOUR]</w> 卸下。你可以在物品栏（<w>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_INVENTORY]</w>）中查看它的属性。
  </nomouse>

  {{
      if you.get_base_mutation_level("horns") > 0 then
          return "注意：由于你长着角，因此无法穿戴头盔。"
                 .. "（按 <w>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_MUTATIONS]</w> 查看你的突变"
                 .. "和先天能力列表。）"
      end
  }}
  ```

- `hint:hint_seen_bazaar_portal`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  这<console>‘$1’</console>是一座通往跨维度集市的传送门，集市里满是商店。如果不尽快进入，传送门就会消失，所以赶快行动。
  ```

- `hint:hint_seen_branch`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  这个<console>“$1”</console>是通往地牢另一个分支的入口。每个分支都有不同的地形和怪物，有些分支还包含通往其他分支的入口。你可以检查这个入口来了解更多信息<localtiles>：将鼠标悬停在它所在的格子上</localtiles><nomouse>：
  按 <w>$cmd[CMD_LOOK_AROUND]</w>，再将光标移到入口上</nomouse>。

  有些分支会比你当前所在的分支安全得多，奖励也更丰厚。另一些则可能非常危险，最好等角色更强大后再前往。
  ```

- `hint:hint_seen_door`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  那<console> $1</console>是一扇关着的门。走向它即可打开。有时把门关上会很有用。站在门旁时，<nomouse>按 <w>$cmd[CMD_CLOSE_DOOR]</w> 即可关门；如果旁边有好几扇门，系统会提示你选择方向。</nomouse><localtiles>单击门即可将它关上。</localtiles>
  ```

- `hint:hint_seen_escape_hatch`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  这<console>‘$1’</console>是逃生舱口。和楼梯类似，你可以分别用 <w>$cmd[CMD_GO_UPSTAIRS]</w> 和 <w>$cmd[CMD_GO_DOWNSTAIRS]</w> 离开当前楼层<localtiles>（也可以<w>单击</w>舱口）</localtiles>；但与楼梯不同，逃生舱口只能单向通行，因此向下使用时要小心！
  ```

- `hint:hint_seen_first_object`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  那个<console>$1 </console>是物品。走到那里并按
  <input>$cmd[CMD_PICKUP]</input><localtiles>，或在物品栏中用<input>左键单击</input>它</localtiles>，即可拾取。<console>物品通常以 <w>%?!\"=()[</w> 之类的非字母符号显示。</console>有些物品<tiles>带有绿色边框，</tiles>会被自动拾取。

  物品进入物品栏后，<localtiles>用一次<input>Shift-左键单击</input>即可把它丢下。每当你用<input>右键单击</input>物品时</localtiles><console>按<input>$cmd[CMD_DROP]</input>即可把它丢下。每当你在物品栏（<input>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_INVENTORY]</input>）中查看物品时</console>，都能阅读它的属性和描述。
  ```

- `hint:hint_seen_gold`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  你捡到了第一堆金币<console>（“<yellow>$item[gold]</yellow>”）</console>。按 <w>$cmd[CMD_LIST_GOLD]</w> 查看你有多少金币。金币可在探索途中发现的商店中使用。
  ```

- `hint:hint_seen_jewellery`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  你捡到了一件珠宝，可能是戒指
  <console>（“<w>$item[jewellery]</w>”）</console>，也可能是项链<console>（“<w>$item[amulet]</w>”）</console>。
  <nomouse>按 <w>$cmd[CMD_WEAR_JEWELLERY]</w> 穿戴，按 <w>$cmd[CMD_REMOVE_JEWELLERY]</w> 卸下。你可以在物品栏（<w>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_INVENTORY]</w>）中查看它的属性。</nomouse>
  <localtiles>单击它即可穿戴，再次单击即可卸下；<w>右键单击</w>可查看它的属性。</localtiles>
  ```

- `hint:hint_seen_misc`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  这确实是件奇特的物品。你可以<localtiles>单击它</localtiles><nomouse>按 <w>$cmd[CMD_EVOKE]</w></nomouse>来摆弄一下，看看它有什么作用。像往常一样，从物品栏（<w>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_INVENTORY]</w>）中选择它，或许能看到更多信息。
  ```

- `hint:hint_seen_missiles`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  这是你捡到的第一叠投掷物<console>（“<w>$item[missile]</w>”）</console>。回旋镖、投掷网等投掷物可以徒手投掷；投掷技能越高，它们就越有效。
  <localtiles><w>右键单击</w></localtiles><nomouse>选择</nomouse>物品栏（<w>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_INVENTORY]</w>）中的该物品，即可查看更多信息。

  {{
      if crawl.hints_type() == "ranger" then
          return "你已经训练了远程武器，因此并不真正需要"
                 .. "另一种远程攻击手段。"
      elseif crawl.hints_type() == "magic" then
          return "不过，作为施法者，你并不真正需要另一种"
                 .. "远程攻击手段。"
      else
          return ""
      end
  }}
  ```

- `hint:hint_seen_portal`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  这<console>‘$1’</console>是一座通往可选楼层的传送门；多一份危险，也会多一份战利品。跳过它不会受到惩罚，但传送门会随着时间消失，所以你必须现在决定是否愿意冒险。
  ```

- `hint:hint_seen_portal_2`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  传送门的用法和楼梯一样：站在上面，<localtiles><w>单击</w>，或</localtiles>按 <w>$cmd[CMD_GO_DOWNSTAIRS]</w> 即可进入。要返回，需找到<tiles>一座外观相似的传送门。</tiles><console>另一个 $1——但绝不能用你出发时所在的那座远古石拱！</console>
  ```

- `hint:hint_seen_potion`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  你捡到了第一瓶药水<console>（“<w>$item[potion]</w>”）</console>。
  <localtiles>只需<w>左键单击</w>它，或按</localtiles>
  <nomouse>按</nomouse> <w>$cmd[CMD_QUAFF]</w> 饮用。

  药水一旦通过鉴定卷轴或饮用得到鉴定，你就会自动认出所有同类药水；因此，有时仅为鉴定而饮用药水也很有用。
  ```

- `hint:hint_seen_runed_door`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  那<console> $1</console>是一扇符文门，门后很可能有格外危险的东西。怪物绝不会打开它。如果你打开，符文就会破碎，它也会变成普通的门。
  ```

- `hint:hint_seen_scroll`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  你捡到了第一张卷轴<console>（“<w>$item[scroll]</w>”）</console>。
  <localtiles>只需<w>左键单击</w>它，或按</localtiles>
  <nomouse>按</nomouse> <w>$cmd[CMD_READ]</w> 阅读。

  卷轴一旦通过鉴定卷轴或阅读得到鉴定，你就会自动认出所有同类卷轴；因此，有时仅为鉴定而阅读卷轴也很有用。
  ```

- `hint:hint_seen_shop`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  那个<console> $1</console>是商店。站在该格上，按
  <w>$cmd[CMD_GO_UPSTAIRS]</w> 或 <w>$cmd[CMD_GO_DOWNSTAIRS]</w>
  <tiles>，或<w>左键单击</w>商店</tiles>，即可进入。

  如果有想买但暂时买不起的东西，可以按住 <w>Shift</w> 并按对应字母，把它加入购物清单。金币足够时，游戏会提醒你购买。
  ```

- `hint:hint_seen_spbook`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  你捡到了一本法术书<console>（“<w>$item[book]</w>”）</console>。拾取时，其中的法术会立刻加入你的法术库。你可以用 <w>$cmd[CMD_MEMORISE_SPELL]</w> 从中记忆法术，并用 <w>$cmd[CMD_CAST_SPELL]</w> 施放。
  {{
      if you.god() == "Trog" then
          return "不过，当你记忆或研习魔法时，特洛格会很生气！"
      end
  }}
  ```

- `hint:hint_seen_staff`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  你捡到了一根法杖<console>（“<w>$item[magical staff]</w>”）</console>。必须先用 <w>$cmd[CMD_WIELD_WEAPON]</w> 持握才能发挥作用。施法者用法杖提高特定法术学派的威力；它也可以当作武器。<localtiles>可<w>左键单击</w>法杖来持握。</localtiles>
  ```

- `hint:hint_seen_trap`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  你刚刚发现了一个陷阱。你偶尔会遇到这种讨厌的机关。
  ```

- `hint:hint_seen_wand`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  你捡到了第一根魔杖<console>（“<w>$item[wand]</w>”）</console>。
  <localtiles>只需<w>左键单击</w>它，或按</localtiles>
  <nomouse>按</nomouse> <w>$cmd[CMD_EVOKE]</w> 激活。

  找到更多同类魔杖时，它们会合并进这根魔杖并为其增加充能。
  ```

- `hint:hint_seen_weapon`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  这是你捡到的第一把武器<console>（“<w>$item[weapon]</w>”）</console>。用 <w>$cmd[CMD_WIELD_WEAPON]</w><localtiles>或<w>左键单击</w>它</localtiles>来持握；但要留意，它对应的武器技能可能和你当前训练的技能不同。你可以从物品栏（<w>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_INVENTORY]</w>）<localtiles>或通过<w>右键单击</w>它</localtiles>查看武器属性。

  {{
      if crawl.hints_type() == "berserk" then
          return "你最好继续使用斧类武器。不过，查看其他斧类武器的"
                 .. "附魔仍可能值得！"
      end
  }}
  ```

- `hint:hint_seen_zero_exp_mon`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  那个<console> $1</console>是$2。严格来说，它属于怪物，但更像地牢里的摆设：既无害，也不会移动。如果挡路，你可以像攻击其他怪物一样杀死它，但不会因此获得任何经验。
  ```

- `hint:hint_skill_raise`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  你的一项技能刚刚达到一个里程碑。每当你通过杀死怪物获得经验值时，正在使用的技能都会自动得到训练。默认情况下，经验值会分配给你正在主动使用的技能。要查看或管理技能，请按 <w>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_SKILLS]</w><localtiles>，或点击右下角的技能标签页</localtiles>。
  ```

- `hint:hint_spell_miscast_effects`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  施法失误可能造成恶劣后果，法术越难尤其如此。你的魔法技能越高，成功施放法术的概率就越大；一些物品也能提高这个概率。使用 <w>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_SPELLS]</w> 命令<localtiles>，或将鼠标悬停在法术图块上</localtiles>，即可查看当前的失败率。
  ```

- `hint:hint_stair_brand`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  <tiles>楼梯地块上的小符号表示该位置还有物品，你可能会想查看。</tiles>
  <console>如果有物品盖在楼梯或逃生舱口上，系统会把 <w><<</w> 或 <w>></w> 符号高亮显示，而不会用物品图符遮住楼梯符号。</console>
  ```

- `hint:hint_temporarily_friendly`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  不过，它只是<w>暂时</w>对你友好；这种友好状态消失后，它会再次变得危险。
  ```

- `hint:hint_trap_brand`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  <console>如果有物品盖在陷阱上，系统会把 <w>^</w> 符号高亮显示，而不会用物品图符遮住陷阱符号。</console>
  ```

- `hint:hint_wield_weapon`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  按 <w>$cmd[CMD_WEAPON_SWAP]</w> 可以轻松切回 a 槽位中的武器。要更改物品槽位，先按 <w>$cmd[CMD_ADJUST_INVENTORY]</w>，再按 <w>i</w>，然后选择合适的槽位。
  ```

- `hint:hint_you_enchanted`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  各种状态效果都可能暂时作用于你。它们的简短说明会显示在属性区域底部。按 <w>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_CHARACTER_STATUS]</w> 可查看详情；按 <w>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_COMMANDS]/T</w> 可搜索完整的状态效果说明。
  ```

- `hint:hint_you_miscast`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  你刚刚发生了一次施法失误。
  ```

- `hint:hint_you_mutated`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  变异有好有坏。变异药水是移除不良变异最常见的办法，但也可能带来新的变异。按 <w>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_MUTATIONS]</w> 查看你的变异。
  ```

- `hint:hint_you_poison`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  中毒会迅速削减你的生命值。你可以按 <w>$cmd[CMD_REST]</w> 休息，等待毒性自行消退；但如果你正在战斗或所中之毒足以致命，最好饮用一瓶治疗药水。
  ```

- `hint:hint_you_resist`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  游戏中的大多数攻击由你的 AC 防御。火焰、吸取等攻击还可由对应抗性进一步减轻；减速、混乱等妖术效果则由意志力抵抗。你会找到能提升这两类抗性的物品。

  要查看当前全部抗性，<localtiles><w>右键点击</w>角色人偶，或</localtiles>按 <w>$cmd[CMD_RESISTS_SCREEN]</w>。
  ```

- `hint:hint_you_silence`

  exact proposed_translation：

  ```text
  处于沉默状态时，你无法施法、阅读卷轴或祈求神力。作用半径内的怪物同样受到限制。沉默场可以通过<tiles>特殊样式的边框地块</tiles><console>颜色不同的地面格</console>辨认；它始终以你为中心并随你移动。其半径会逐渐缩小，最终只影响你一人，然后彻底消失。
  ```

### defer implementation（2）

- `hint:dissection reminder`

  - 结论：`defer implementation`。
  - deferral reason：该 identity 只有 ZH 测试消费且正文依赖失去生产入口的旧尸体机制
  - owner：crawl-coder/zh-code-reviewer
  - re-entry trigger：测试获得不承载旧玩法的稳定 fixture，或游戏重新引入有权威 EN 的 producer。
  - actual behavior：测试设当前神为 Trog，通过 crawl.test_hint_text() 查询 ZH HintsDB；仅断言中文神名且无原始 Trog。正常游戏无查询。
  - reviewer rationale：该 key 没有 EN 条目或生产 producer；ZH 正文描述旧尸体机制，目前仅被 ZH runtime 测试用于验证动态神名。没有英文或生产语境时不能凭空重译。
  - rejected alternatives：
    - 反向编造 EN：违反边界。
    - 按旧尸体机制润色：误当当前事实。
    - 直接删除：破坏测试。

- `hint:hint_fleeing_monster`

  - 结论：`defer implementation`。
  - deferral reason：TAG34 compatibility enum unconsumed；当前没有可验证的显示路径。
  - owner：crawl-coder／兼容生命周期维护者
  - re-entry trigger：恢复当前 producer/consumer，或明确决定维护并验证 TAG34 历史兼容文本时重新审阅。
  - actual behavior：当前版本不产生或显示此提示；只保留 TAG_MAJOR_VERSION == 34 下的兼容枚举和 EN/ZH TextDB 条目。
  - reviewer rationale：现译把“查看石头说明”改成直接使用 CMD_FIRE，确有语义和 token 差异；但该 identity 目前仅为 TAG34 兼容枚举，没有现行 producer 或 display consumer，不能将历史文本当作当前行为落地。
  - rejected alternatives：
    - retranslate：在没有当前消费者时直接修改历史兼容文本，缺少明确的运行时验收对象。
    - keep：现译额外引入 CMD_FIRE，不能视为与英文完全一致。

<w>$1</w>）</console>前活下来的机会，要一直移动，不要和任何怪物交战，也不要去追逐地上的物品。如果怪物正在逼近，试着使用物品和能力脱身。","reentry_trigger":"当该 TextDB 值、producer、consumer、lifecycle、token contract 或 docs/glossary.md 权威发生变化时重新审阅。","rejected_alternatives":["keep：会保留 console 残句及多项生存建议缺失。","adjust：缺失覆盖整段，局部增补不如完整重译清晰。"],"reviewer_rationale":"现译第一段基本对应，但第二段错误地置于仅 Tiles 显示的标签内，使 console 句子残缺；并遗漏‘一直移动’‘不要追逐地上物品’‘怪物逼近时用物品和能力脱身’三项关键生存建议。","terminal_conclusion":"retranslate"}

{"actual_behavior":"系统扫描视野内匹配 auto_exclude 的敌对怪物；通常要求怪物固定不动，睡眠中的可移动怪物也可能符合。自动禁区在原怪物消失、不可见或不再符合条件时清除；只手动清除而不改选项时可再次建立。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:509","lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","lua":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","substitution":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"},"current_chinese":"为了防止自动旅行或自动探索带你进入危险区域，你可以通过进入地图视图\n（<w>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_MAP]</w>）然后在怪物位置上用 <w>e</w> 切换排除半径\n来设置旅行排除区域。为了简化操作，一些不移动的怪物会自动被排除。","current_english":"To prevent autotravel or autoexplore taking you into dangerous territory, you\ncan set travel exclusions by entering the map view (<w>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_MAP]</w>)\nand then toggling the exclusion radius on the monster position with <w>e</w>.\nTo make this easier, some immobile monsters listed in the <w>auto_exclude</w>\noption (such as this one) are considered dangerous enough to warrant an\nautomatic setting of an exclusion. It will be automatically cleared if you\nmanage to kill the monster. You could also manually remove the exclusion with\n<w>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_MAP]ee</w> but unless you remove this monster from the\nauto_exclude list, the exclusion will be reset the next turn.","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"interface and progression","display_context":"Hints/tutorial message stream through print_hint.","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:1375","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/hints.txt:610","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/hints.txt:172","crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:509","crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"],"fact_sha256":"707b8462a56f01429a5b172cccc9419de84133470266d99192288c6822abc563","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md <w>$cmd[CMD_MAP_EXCLUDE_AREA]</w> 切换排除半径。为了简化操作，<w>auto_exclude</w> 选项列出的一些无法移动的怪物（例如眼前这个）会被视为足够危险，系统会自动为它们设置禁区。杀死怪物后，该禁区会自动清除。你也可以按 <w>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_MAP]</w> 进入地图，再按两次 <w>$cmd[CMD_MAP_EXCLUDE_AREA]</w> 手动移除禁区；但除非把这种怪物从 <w>auto_exclude</w> 列表中移除，否则下一回合禁区还会重新出现。","reentry_trigger":"当该 TextDB 值、producer、consumer、lifecycle、token contract 或 docs/glossary.md 权威发生变化时重新审阅。","rejected_alternatives":["keep：会遗漏禁区生命周期和命令后果。","adjust：缺失超过一半命题，完整重译更可靠。","沿用固定键 e 或 `$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_MAP]ee`：无法随玩家键位重映射。"],"reviewer_rationale":"现译只保留设置禁区的前半部分，遗漏 auto_exclude 选项限定、当前怪物示例、击杀后自动清除、手动移除命令及下一回合重新设置等重要条件和后果。修正后的权威 EN 还明确要求 CMD_DISPLAY_MAP 与 CMD_MAP_EXCLUDE_AREA 保持为独立、可重映射的命令 token，不能写死 e 或把 ee 拼进命令标识。","terminal_conclusion":"retranslate"}
{"actual_behavior":"默认 explore_greedy 开启时，自动探索会寻找未探索区域并兼顾有趣物品；旅行逻辑遇到怪物、危险地形、符文门和配置的有趣地形时会停止或重新选路。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:509","lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","lua":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","substitution":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"},"current_chinese":"完全探索一个楼层并捡起所有看起来有趣的物品可能很乏味。\n为了节省这种乏味，你可以按 <w>$cmd[CMD_EXPLORE]</w> 来自动探索，\n它将自动探索未映射的区域，自动捡起有趣的物品，并在有怪物出现时停下来。","current_english":"Fully exploring a level and picking up all the interesting looking items can be\ntedious. To save on this tedium you can press <w>$cmd[CMD_EXPLORE]</w> to\nauto-explore, which will automatically explore unmapped regions, automatically\npick up interesting items, and stop if a monster or interesting dungeon feature\n(stairs, altar, etc.) is encountered.","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"movement and exploration","display_context":"Hints/tutorial message {"actual_behavior":"信仰特洛格、当前可以狂暴且尚未熟悉该能力时，看见怪物有四分之一概率触发；特洛格的被动效果可在狂暴击杀时按虔诚度概率延长持续时间。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:509","lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","lua":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","substitution":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"},"current_chinese":"面对特别难对付的敌人时，你应该使用你的狂暴\n<w>$cmd[CMD_USE_ABILITY]</w>能力。在狂暴期间击杀怪物会让它持续更久。","current_english":"Against particularly difficult foes, you should use your Berserk\n<w>$cmd[CMD_USE_ABILITY]</w>bility. {"actual_behavior":"皈依艾申扎利时专用分支显示；现行神祇说明确认探索时提供诅咒、诅咒装备强化技能并给予其他知识性帮助。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:509","lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","lua":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","substitution":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"},"current_chinese":"你刚刚皈依了 <w>阿申扎里</w>。与其他神不同，阿申扎里只关心诅咒。\n当你探索地牢时，阿申扎里会提供诅咒，将一件物品锁定在你身上。\n你穿戴的诅咒物品越多，阿申扎里就会越多地增强你的技能和其他属性。","current_english":"You've just converted to worshipping <w>Ashenzari</w>. Unlike other gods,\nAshenzari only cares about curses. As you explore the dungeon, Ashenzari will\noffer curses, locking an item onto you. The more cursed items you wear, the more\nAshenzari will enhance your skills and otherwise help you.","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"gods and abilities","display_context":"Hints/tutorial message stream through print_hint.","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:1462","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/hints.txt:146","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/hints.txt:236","crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:509","crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"],"fact_sha256":"561c106fcf39834ef760a83b113450bbb7b8b1dc8002fe32637d23a9f19fe054","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 95eeacf9704e046c2010ef34859b750d2f8a1937ad87c4a86e8a404c98689407","identity":"hint:hint_convert ashenzari","lifecycle":"current-producer","producer":[{"function":"print_hint","kind":"literal","location":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:1462","lookup_key":"HINT_CONVERT Ashenzari"}],"production_facts":{"chinese":"你刚刚皈依了 <w>阿申扎里</w>。与其他神不同，阿申扎里只关心诅咒。\n当你探索地牢时，阿申扎里会提供诅咒，将一件物品锁定在你身上。\n你穿戴的诅咒物品越多，阿申扎里就会越多地增强你的技能和其他属性。","chinese_key_line":236,"chinese_raw_key":"HINT_CONVERT Ashenzari","chinese_tokens":{"command_ascii_adjacencies":[],"command_counts":{},"commands":[],"item_counts":{},"items":[],"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"malformed_commands":[],"malformed_items":[],"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"platform_counts":{},"platform_sequence":[],"tag_counts":{"</w>":1,"<w>":1},"tag_sequence":["<w>","</w>"],"unknown_tags":[]},"placeholder_counts":{},"placeholders":[],"unknown_commands":[],"unknown_dollar_tokens":[],"unknown_items":[]},"consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:509","lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","lua":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","substitution":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"},"dependency_group":"gods and abilities","display_context":"Hints/tutorial message stream through print_hint.","english":"You've just converted to worshipping <w>Ashenzari</w>. {"actual_behavior":"皈依入时专用显示；入会随探索提供永久舍弃，舍弃带来虔诚值并强化能力。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:509","lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","lua":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","substitution":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"},"current_chinese":"你刚刚皈依了 <w>鲁</w>。与其他神不同，鲁以永久牺牲为交换给予虔诚值\n（神眷），比如失去训练某些技能的能力，或永久失去一只手。\n你的虔诚值越多，鲁就会给予越多能力，你的力量也会越强。","current_english":"You've just converted to worshipping <w>Ru</w>. Unlike other gods, Ru grants\npiety (divine favour) in exchange for permanent sacrifices, like losing the\nability to train certain skills, or permanently losing a hand. The more piety\nyou have, the more abilities Ru will grant, and the more powerful those\nabilities will become. {"actual_behavior":"皈依乌斯卡亚时专用显示；神眷由战斗伤害快速累积，脱离战斗后快速下降。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:509","lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","lua":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","substitution":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"},"current_chinese":"你刚刚皈依了 <w>乌斯卡约</w>。与其他神不同，乌斯卡约的神眷无法在\n你探索地牢或杀死怪物时逐渐获得。相反，乌斯卡约的虔诚值是在战斗中\n随着你造成伤害而迅速获得的。你在战斗中停留的时间越长，造成的伤害越多，\n虔诚值积累得就越快。","current_english":"You've just converted to worshipping <w>Uskayaw</w>. Unlike other gods,\nUskayaw's favour cannot be gradually gained as you explore the dungeon or slay\nmonsters. Instead, piety with Uskayaw is gained rapidly in combat as you deal\ndamage. The longer you remain in combat and the more damage you deal, the\ngreater the powers you will be offered. Once combat ends, Uskayaw's favour will\nbe quickly lost, and must be regained in the next battle.","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"gods and abilities","display_context":"Hints/tutorial message stream through print_hint.","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:1468","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/hints.txt:168","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/hints.txt:253","crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:509","crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"],"fact_sha256":"befb6e9a51c78bb6125ceac45a086be6bc151d8ae3ceda43700daa823444bba5","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 95eeacf9704e046c2010ef34859b750d2f8a1937ad87c4a86e8a404c98689407","identity":"hint:hint_convert uskayaw","lifecycle":"current-producer","producer":[{"function":"print_hint","kind":"literal","location":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:1468","lookup_key":"HINT_CONVERT Uskayaw"}],"production_facts":{"chinese":"你刚刚皈依了 <w>乌斯卡约</w>。与其他神不同，乌斯卡约的神眷无法在\n你探索地牢或杀死怪物时逐渐获得。相反，乌斯卡约的虔诚值是在战斗中\n随着你造成伤害而迅速获得的。你在战斗中停留的时间越长，造成的伤害越多，\n虔诚值积累得就越快。","chinese_key_line":253,"chinese_raw_key":"HINT_CONVERT Uskayaw","chinese_tokens":{"command_ascii_adjacencies":[],"command_counts":{},"commands":[],"item_counts":{},"items":[],"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"malformed_commands":[],"malformed_items":[],"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"platform_counts":{},"platform_sequence":[],"tag_counts":{"</w>":1,"<w>":1},"tag_sequence":["<w>","</w>"],"unknown_tags":[]},"placeholder_counts":{},"placeholders":[],"unknown_commands":[],"unknown_dollar_tokens":[],"unknown_items":[]},"consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:509","lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","lua":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","substitution":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"},"dependency_group":"gods and abilities","display_context":"Hints/tutorial message stream through print_hint.","english":"You've just converted to worshipping <w>Uskayaw</w>. {"actual_behavior":"皈依佐姆时专用显示；当前神祇说明同样强调无法影响佐姆的心情。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:509","lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","lua":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","substitution":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"},"current_chinese":"你刚刚皈依了 <w>佐姆</w>。作为佐姆的追随者，你会被当作玩物对待，\n为了佐姆自己的娱乐而随机被帮助或伤害。\n\n没有任何方法能保证佐姆保持好心情——任何追随者所能期望的最好结果\n就是避免让佐姆感到无聊。","current_english":"You've just converted to worshipping <w>Xom</w>. {"actual_behavior":"自动拾取因附近不可见危险而关闭时，Hints 模式记录最近遇到隐形怪物并显示本警告。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:509","lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","lua":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","substitution":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"},"current_chinese":"与你无法看到的怪物战斗是困难的。如果你没有看见隐形的来源，\n你可以通过将隐形怪物引诱到浅水、不透明的云雾（来自迷雾卷轴）\n或走廊中来找到它们，在那里它们的移动将更容易预测。\n如果怪物足够弱小，你可能可以简单地承受它的攻击。","current_english":"Fighting against a monster you cannot see is difficult. If you don't have a\nsource of see invisibility, you can find invisible monsters by luring them into\nshallow water, opaque clouds (from a scroll of fog), or a corridor, where their\nmovements will be more predictable. If things go south, try teleporting out, or\nleaving the level entirely!","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"interface and progression","display_context":"Hints/tutorial message stream through print_hint.","evidence_locations":["crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:1391","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/hints.txt:650","crawl-ref/source/dat/descript/zh/hints.txt:348","crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:509","crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"],"fact_sha256":"797b9cdaeecf25dbe12e33eadf6c39a17ea4bf8f9ca985198190f5cde99e9218","glossary_authority":"docs/glossary.md SHA-256 95eeacf9704e046c2010ef34859b750d2f8a1937ad87c4a86e8a404c98689407","identity":"hint:hint_invisible_danger","lifecycle":"current-producer","producer":[{"function":"print_hint","kind":"literal","location":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:1391","lookup_key":"HINT_INVISIBLE_DANGER"}],"production_facts":{"chinese":"与你无法看到的怪物战斗是困难的。如果你没有看见隐形的来源，\n你可以通过将隐形怪物引诱到浅水、不透明的云雾（来自迷雾卷轴）\n或走廊中来找到它们，在那里它们的移动将更容易预测。\n如果怪物足够弱小，你可能可以简单地承受它的攻击。","chinese_key_line":348,"chinese_raw_key":"HINT_INVISIBLE_DANGER","chinese_tokens":{"command_ascii_adjacencies":[],"command_counts":{},"commands":[],"item_counts":{},"items":[],"lua":{"blocks":[],"errors":[]},"malformed_commands":[],"malformed_items":[],"markup":{"balance_errors":[],"malformed":[],"platform_counts":{},"platform_sequence":[],"tag_counts":{},"tag_sequence":[],"unknown_tags":[]},"placeholder_counts":{},"placeholders":[],"unknown_commands":[],"unknown_dollar_tokens":[],"unknown_items":[]},"consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:509","lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","lua":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","substitution":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"},"dependency_group":"interface and progression","display_context":"Hints/tutorial message stream through print_hint.","english":"Fighting against a monster you cannot see is difficult. {"actual_behavior":"Hints 模式施法失误且最大法力大于 2 时显示；低严重度失误只有污染/无害效果，高严重度可能产生学派副作用，失败仍已支付完整法力。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:509","lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","lua":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","substitution":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"},"current_chinese":"如果施法成功率高（可以通过输入 <w>$cmd[CMD_CAST_SPELL]\\?</w> 或\n<w>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_SPELLS]</w> 来检查），那么施法失败仅仅意味着\n法术不起作用，并伴有无害的副作用。\n然而，对于失败率高的法术，结果可能更严重。","current_english":"If the spellcasting success chance is high (which can be checked by entering\n<w>$cmd[CMD_CAST_SPELL]\\?</w> or <w>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_SPELLS]</w>) then a miscast\nmerely means the spell is not working, along with a harmless side effect.\nHowever, for spells with a high failure rate, there's a chance of contaminating\nyourself with magical energy, plus a chance of an additional harmful side\neffect. Normally this isn't a problem, since magical contamination bleeds off\nover time, but if you're repeatedly contaminated in a short amount of time\nyou'll mutate or suffer from other ill side effects.\n\nNote that a miscast spell will still consume the full amount of MP that a\nsuccessfully cast spell would.","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"magic and status","display_context":"Hints/tutorial message stream through entering\n<w>$cmd[CMD_CAST_SPELL]\\?</w> or <w>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_SPELLS]</w>) then a miscast\nmerely means the spell is not working, along with a harmless side effect.\nHowever, for spells with a high <w>$cmd[CMD_CAST_SPELL]\\?</w> 或\n<w>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_SPELLS]</w> 查看），那么施法失误只会使法术未能生效，并伴随一个无害的副作用。\n但失败率较高的法术在施法失误时，可能使你受到魔法能量污染，并可能额外产生一个有害副作用。通常这并不是问题，因为魔法污染会随时间消退；但如果你在短时间内反复受到污染，就会发生变异或遭受其他有害影响。\n\n请注意，施法失误仍会消耗与成功施放该法术时相同的全部法力。","reentry_trigger":"当该 TextDB 值、producer、consumer、lifecycle、token contract 或 docs/glossary.md 权威发生变化时重新审阅。","rejected_alternatives":["“结果可能更严重”不能替代被删去的条件、后果和法力消耗事实。"],"reviewer_rationale":"现译只保留开头，漏掉污染概率、有害附加效果、自然消退、短时间反复污染的后果，以及失误仍消耗全部 MP；miscast 术语也错误。","terminal_conclusion":"retranslate"}

{"actual_behavior":"处于安全环境且生命或法力低于一半时调用治疗提醒；若近期没有隐形怪物记录，则显示本键。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:509","lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","lua":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","substitution":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"},"current_chinese":"如果你的生命值或魔力不足，并且没有紧急移动的需要，你可以休息一会儿。\n理想情况下，你应该撤退到一个你已经探索并清理了怪物的区域再休息，\n因为在已探索地形边缘休息会增加休息被怪物打断的风险。","current_english":"If you're low on health or magic and there's no urgent need to move, you can\nrest for a bit. Ideally, you should retreat to an area you've already explored\nand cleared of monsters before resting, since resting on the edge of the\nexplored terrain increases the risk of rest being interrupted by a wandering\nmonster. {"actual_behavior":"安全恢复检查中，若最近二十回合内记录过隐形怪物，则显示本专用提醒并更新时间戳。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:509","lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","lua":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","substitution":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"},"current_chinese":"你最近注意到了一个隐形怪物，所以除非你杀死了它或离开了现场，\n休息可能不安全。如果你仍然需要补充生命值或魔力，你将不得不喝适当的药水。\n为了正常休息，你首先必须远离危险。","current_english":"You recently noticed an invisible monster, so unless you killed it or left the\nscene, resting might not be safe. If you still need to replenish your health or\nmagic, you'll have to quaff an appropriate potion. {"actual_behavior":"玩家被施加 DUR_STICKY_FLAME 时触发；主动移动会大幅削减持续时间，进入水中会立即熄灭，但来源可再次施加效果。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:509","lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","lua":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","substitution":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"},"current_chinese":"你着火了！火焰每回合都会造成伤害。移动会帮助火焰更快熄灭，\n踏入水中则会完全熄灭它。如果你不动，火焰也会自行熄灭，\n但那样你需要承受更多伤害。","current_english":"You have been covered in sticky liquid flame. You can put this out more quickly\nby spending your turns moving, or put it out entirely by walking into a nearby\npool of water. {"actual_behavior":"作为低生命 HINT_RUN_AWAY 的固定尾段显示；游戏允许符合条件的相邻怪物在玩家开始上下楼时跟随，甩掉的怪物仍留在原楼层。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:509","lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","lua":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","substitution":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"},"current_chinese":"当战斗变得艰难时，记住总是有出口的！在开阔地带与怪物战斗\n意味着你面前总是有逃跑路线。在死胡同中战斗是危险的。\n通过从已经清理了怪物的区域撤回，你可以休息并补满生命值和魔力，\n然后以更有利的条件重新接战。","current_english":"If retreating to another level, keep in mind that monsters may follow you if\nthey're standing right next to you when you start climbing or descending the\nstairs. {"actual_behavior":"观察到祭坛格时触发；console 注入 glyph，tiles 放置祭坛标签。嵌入 Lua 仅在无信仰且 magic hints 类型时追加建议。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:509","lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","lua":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","substitution":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"},"current_chinese":"你发现了一个祭坛！祭坛允许你向一位神献身，\n他们会以强大的神圣能力回报你的虔诚服务。不同的神有不同的喜好和厌恶。","current_english":"That<console> $1</console> is an altar.\n<localtiles>By <w>right-clicking</w> on it with your mouse</localtiles>\n<nomouse>If you target the altar with <w>$cmd[CMD_LOOK_AROUND]</w>, then press\n<w>$cmd[CMD_TARGET_DESCRIBE]</w></nomouse> you can get a short description.\n\nPress <w>$cmd[CMD_GO_UPSTAIRS]</w> or <w>$cmd[CMD_GO_DOWNSTAIRS]</w> while\nstanding on the square to join the faith or read some information about the god\nin question. Before taking up the corresponding faith you'll be asked for\nconfirmation.\n\n{{\n    if you.god() == \"No God\" and crawl.hints_type() == \"magic\" then\n        return \"The simplest god for an inexperienced conjurer is probably \"\n               .. \"Vehumet, though many other gods, like Sif Muna, can also \"\n               .. \"work well.\"\n    end\n}}","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"interface and progression","display_context":"Hints/tutorial message stream through {"actual_behavior":"首次看到普通下楼梯时触发；跨层到达后此前未见怪物跳过首次行动。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:509","lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","lua":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","substitution":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"},"current_chinese":"这些<console>“$1”</console>是下楼梯。你可以沿它们向下进入下一（更深）层\n（<w>$cmd[CMD_GO_DOWNSTAIRS]</w>）。要返回本层，请站在上楼梯上按\n<w>$cmd[CMD_GO_UPSTAIRS]</w>。使用楼梯后，在任何你此前未见过的怪物行动前，\n你必定有一次行动机会；之后怪物会先行动。\n\n<localtiles>或者，你可以站在楼梯上<w>左键单击</w>它来使用。</localtiles>","current_english":"These<console> '$1'</console> are downstairs. When\nyou take stairs, you are guaranteed a opportunity to act before any monsters you\nhave not seen before; after that, monsters get to move first.\n\n<localtiles>Alternatively, you can <w>left-click</w> on stairs you're standing\non to use them.</localtiles>","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"movement and exploration","display_context":"Hints/tutorial {"actual_behavior":"首次看见 is_firewood 的零经验怪物时触发；消费时用该怪物的字形替换 $1、名称替换 $2。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:509","lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","lua":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","substitution":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"},"current_chinese":"那个怪物不提供经验值，因为它太弱了，无法提供真正的挑战。\n并非所有战斗都值得打。","current_english":"That<console> $1</console> is $2. {"actual_behavior":"提示模式开局读取该 key，执行嵌入 Lua，将当前种族和背景翻译为 ZH 显示名；随后替换 $cmd[...] 为当前绑定键，并在弹窗中展示。","confidence":"high","consumer":{"display":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:405","lookup":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2588","lua":"crawl-ref/source/database.cc:2579","substitution":"crawl-ref/source/hints.cc:328"},"current_chinese":"<white>欢迎你来到地牢爬行者的游戏世界！</white>\n\n你的任务是扮演<w>{{ return crawl.t_(you.race()) .. \" \" .. crawl.t_(you.class()) }}</w>\n角色深入地城深处，找到传说中的佐特之球并带着它返回地面。不过，在刚开始玩的时候，\n你只要探索地城就好。尽你所能层层推进，不过要小心：死亡的危险无处不在。\n\n目前，你只需要记住以下按键的功能：\n  <white>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_COMMANDS]?</white> — 显示物品及命令\n  <white>$cmd[CMD_SAVE_GAME]</white>  — 保存游戏，以便以后继续目前的进度（注意：\n如果死了，就没法重来了）\n  <white>$cmd[CMD_LOOK_AROUND]</white>  — 调查附近的某个物体\n\n提示模式可以帮助你进行游戏而不用阅读游戏说明。如果你还没有玩过教程关卡，\n建议你先尝试通过教程关。当然，如果你想对这个游戏有深入的了解，你可以在文档\n目录\n中阅读以下文档以获取更加详细的游戏资料（所有这些资料在游戏内也能阅读）：\n  <lightblue>quickstart.txt</lightblue>     — 简短的游戏入门说明。\n  <lightblue>crawl_manual.txt</lightblue>   — 包括种族、魔法、\n技能等等的详细说明。\n  <lightblue>options_guide.txt</lightblue>  — 游戏设置详解。\n地牢爬行者的界面有很多地方是可以让玩家自由设置的。\n\n祝你游戏愉快！","current_english":"<white>Welcome to Dungeon Crawl!</white>\n\nYour objective is to lead your <w>{{ return you.race() }} {{ return you.class()\n}}</w> safely through the depths of the dungeon, retrieving the fabled Orb of\nZot and returning it to the surface. In the beginning, however, let discovery\nbe your main goal. Try to delve as deeply as possible but beware; death lurks\naround every corner.\n\nFor the moment, just remember the following keys and their functions:\n  <input>$cmd[CMD_DISPLAY_COMMANDS]?</input> — shows the items and the commands\n  <input>$cmd[CMD_SAVE_GAME]</input>  — saves the game, to be resumed later\n(but note that death is permanent)\n  <input>$cmd[CMD_LOOK_AROUND]</input>  — examines something in your vicinity\n\nThe hint mode will help you play Crawl without reading any documentation. If\nyou haven't yet, you might want to try out the tutorial. Also, if you feel\nintrigued, there is more information available in the following files from the\ndocs/ directory (all of which can also be read in-game):\n  <lightblue>quickstart.txt</lightblue>     — A very short guide to Crawl.\n  <lightblue>crawl_manual.txt</lightblue>   — This contains all details on\nspecies, magic, skills, etc.\n  <lightblue>options_guide.txt</lightblue>  — Crawl's interface is highly\nconfigurable. This document explains all the options.\n\nHappy Crawling!","deferral_owner":null,"deferral_reason":null,"dependency_group":"entry, death, and completion","display_context":"Hints starting-screen popup after DB lookup and static
